### [1] [Recent benchmarks in the Analysis Grand Challenge and integration with Combine (and HS3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19753)
*Peter Fackeldey,Massimiliano Galli,Hannah Havel,Alexander Held,Iason Krommydas,Oksana Shadura,Nick Smith*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高能物理（HEP）分析中的Analysis Grand Challenge（AGC）项目，重点展示其基于现代Python生态（如coffea、uproot、awkward-array）的端到端分析流程，并推动Combine工具与PyHEP生态的集成，同时规划支持HS3标准以实现跨框架、语言无关的统计模型序列化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合CMS主流统计工具Combine与新兴PyHEP Python生态之间的鸿沟，提升分析可复现性、互操作性与性能；并为长期支持HS3标准奠定基础。

Method: 通过AGC作为参考实现，开展Combine与coffea-casa等PyHEP基础设施的集成开发；执行性能基准测试以量化数据处理包优化效果；设计并推进HS3在Combine中的序列化接口实现。

Result: 实现了Combine在coffea-casa上的可运行集成示例；给出了coffea/uproot/awkward性能优化带来的实际加速比；初步构建了HS3与Combine兼容的数据交换原型。

Conclusion: AGC与Combine的协同演进标志着HEP分析正迈向模块化、标准化与跨工具链互操作的新阶段；HS3将成为连接不同统计框架（如RooFit、pyhf、zfit）的关键中间表示。

Abstract: The Analysis Grand Challenge (AGC) showcases an example of HEP analysis. Its reference implementation uses modern Python packages to realize the main steps, from data access to statistical model building and fitting. The packages used for data handling and processing (coffea, uproot, awkward-array) have recently undergone a series of performance optimizations. While not being part of the HEP Python (PyHEP) ecosystem, the Combine tool is a pillar of CMS analyses, covering more than 90% of the analyses published in the last few years. As such, it is necessary to have Combine integrated in the PyHEP ecosystem, using the AGC as example. This project also includes, in the long-term, providing support and integration for the High Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard (HS3), as a way to have a language-independent way of representing the likelihood and use different frameworks interchangeably. In these proceedings we cover part of the recent work performed on the AGC and Combine, including: performance benchmarks, covering benefits introduced by the recent improvements in the data processing packages; examples of how Combine can be integrated and run in a dedicated infrastructure (coffea-casa); and examples and plans to integrate HS3 in Combine.

</details>


### [2] [Status and Prospects of the HEP Statistical Inference Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19761)
*Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了高能物理（HEP）中统计推断工具的现状与未来发展方向，重点讨论RooFit/RooStats框架在面对复杂模型、大规模似然优化及跨框架互操作性挑战时的演进路径，包括ROOT中RooFit自动微分、与JAX等现代库的集成，以及HS3协议推动的框架间通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对LHC实验中统计模型复杂度激增（百量级POI、千量级NP）、大规模似然极小化对可扩展性能的需求，以及与机器学习生态（如JAX、GPU加速框架）和异构硬件的互操作性压力。

Method: 通过升级ROOT/RooFit核心（引入自动微分）、构建跨框架接口（如HS3协议）、实现与JAX等函数式微分库的协同计算，提升统计建模灵活性与计算效率。

Result: 实现了RooFit对自动微分的支持，初步验证了与JAX的互操作能力，并推进HS3作为标准化统计对象序列化与交换协议，为多框架联合拟合与分布式计算奠定基础。

Conclusion: HEP统计工具正从传统C++/RooFit单一体系向模块化、可微分、跨框架互操作的新范式迁移，以支撑未来高维参数空间、AI增强分析及异构计算环境下的精确物理测量。

Abstract: Statistical inference is a crucial part of HEP analyses. Historically based on RooFit and RooStats, the statistical tools used by the experiments are now facing unprecedented challenges, such as the rapidly growing complexity of statistical models (involving hundreds of parameters of interest and thousands of nuisance parameters), the need for scalable performance in large likelihood minimizations, and the demand for interoperability across an increasingly diverse ecosystem of tools, computational hardware, and frameworks and libraries, especially the ones developed and used within the machine learning world. This contribution summarizes status and future plans for the statistical tools used by some of the main LHC experiments (CMS, ATLAS), with a focus on improvements coming from the ROOT world (RooFit automatic differentiation), interoperability with modern libraries (JAX) and communication across frameworks (HS3).

</details>


### [3] [Constraints on the Higgs boson total width from on-shell signal-background interference in the $H\toγγ$ decay channel with $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19948)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过测量Higgs玻色子在γγ末态的共振-连续谱干涉效应，首次实现了对总衰变宽度Γ_H的直接、on-shell实验限制，得到95%置信度上限Γ_H < 307 MeV（预期258 MeV），约为SM预言（4.1 MeV）的75倍；该方法不依赖Higgs耦合假设，对BSM不可见衰变具有独特敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型（SM）Higgs总衰变宽度Γ_H = 4.1 MeV的预言，并探测可能存在的超出标准模型（BSM）不可见或非标准衰变道——任何BSM衰变均会增大Γ_H，故直接测量Γ_H是探针新物理的关键途径。

Method: 利用H → γγ共振峰与连续谱背景之间的量子干涉效应：该干涉导致m_γγ分布的线形畸变，其特征直接依赖于Γ_H，且不预设Higgs与SM粒子的耦合结构（即model-independent）；基于ATLAS探测器在√s = 13 TeV、140 fb⁻¹ LHC Run 2数据中对 diphoton 不变量质量谱的高精度拟合。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，测得Γ_H的观测上限为307 MeV，预期上限为258 MeV；该结果比SM预言大~75倍，是首个基于on-shell干涉的直接Γ_H测量，显著弱于间接off-shell约束但具备完全不同的系统误差来源和模型无关性。

Conclusion: 该测量提供了对Higgs总宽度的新型、模型无关约束，是对现有off-shell方法的重要补充；其灵敏度虽暂未挑战SM，但验证了干涉线形分析这一关键方法论，为未来高亮度LHC及HL-LHC提升Γ_H测量精度奠定了基础。

Abstract: The total decay width of the Higgs boson, $Γ_H$, encodes all of its decay modes; any decay into particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) would increase $Γ_H$ above the SM prediction of 4.1 MeV. In this Letter, a direct, on-shell measurement of $Γ_H$ is reported, exploiting the interference between the resonant $H \to γγ$ amplitude and the continuum diphoton background, which distorts the diphoton invariant mass distribution in a way that depends on $Γ_H$ without requiring assumptions on the Higgs boson couplings. Using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC, an observed (expected) upper limit of $Γ_H < 307$ (258) MeV is set at 95% confidence level, approximately 75 (63) times the SM prediction. Unlike indirect off-shell constraints on $Γ_H$, this measurement does not rely on assumptions about the Higgs boson couplings, providing a novel complementary probe sensitive to potential Higgs boson decays into new particles.

</details>


### [4] [IRIS-HEP 2026 Statistical Ecosystem Blueprint White Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19992)
*Matthew Feickert,Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了高能物理（HEP）领域当前统计工具生态系统的现状，并基于2026年2月IRIS-HEP统计生态系统蓝图会议所汇集的共识，提出了未来研发方向的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对HEP实验数据规模与复杂性持续增长带来的统计分析挑战，需系统评估现有工具生态并协调未来R&D路径。

Method: 通过IRIS-HEP组织的跨机构协作蓝图会议，开展专家调研、工具盘点与需求分析，形成共识性技术路线图。

Result: 梳理出当前HEP主流统计工具（如RooFit、HistFactory、pyhf、TensorFlow Probability等）的适用范围、互操作性瓶颈及维护状态；识别出可扩展性、不确定性建模、机器学习集成和教育推广四大关键发展方向。

Conclusion: HEP统计生态系统需向模块化、标准化与可持续维护演进；强调社区驱动开发、FAIR原则实践及与通用科学计算生态（如SciPy、JAX）深度协同的必要性。

Abstract: This white paper presents the current status of the ecosystem of statistical tools used in High Energy Physics (HEP), and attempts to summarize the views on the future R&D directions. These views have been collected during the latest IRIS-HEP statistical ecosystem blueprint, which took place in February 2026.

</details>


### [5] [Coverage Is Not Ordering: Ancillary Leakage and Representation Dependence in Covariance-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20007)
*Tommaso Dorigo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用协方差矩阵作为测量统计模型代理所引发的深层推断问题，指出该近似会改变似然比排序，进而影响置信决策；作者通过可解的相关测量模型揭示信息分量与辅助残差的分离，并量化了因协方差依赖建模导致的接受域对称差概率——即重复实验中置信判断发生改变的比例；结果表明：在小不确定性极限下，排序偏差为一阶量，而传统覆盖误差仅为二阶；在‘覆盖盲点’处，覆盖误差消失但排序偏差仍存在；文中还将Peelle’s Pertinent Puzzle统一纳入经典辅助统计量（ancillarity）与相关子集（relevant subsets）框架，并推广至非等精度与多测量情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示协方差矩阵作为统计模型代理时对似然推断结构的破坏性影响，尤其关注其对置信序（likelihood-ratio ordering）与覆盖性质（coverage）的系统性偏差，弥补现有高斯近似与误差传播方法在基础推断层面的理论缺位。

Method: 基于可解的关联测量模型，严格分离信息分量（informative component）与辅助残差（ancillary residual），运用精确似然推断（exact likelihood inference）、条件推断（ancillary conditioning）及接受域对称差测度（symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions）量化排序偏差；结合小参数展开分析主导阶行为，并将Peelle’s Pertinent Puzzle嵌入经典辅助统计量理论框架。

Result: 1) 协方差矩阵依赖型建模会引入本应被忽略的辅助残差，导致似然比排序改变；2) 排序偏差量级为总相对不确定度的一阶项，而标准覆盖缺陷为二阶；3) 在覆盖盲点处，覆盖误差消失但排序偏差非零；4) 对于10%总相对不确定度的典型盲例，约7.6%实验的置信决策发生改变；5) Peelle’s Puzzle本质是协方差近似赋予原本辅助的拟合优度统计量以推断相关性。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵不能无害地替代完整统计模型；其引入的似然序扰动是根本性的、一阶的且不可通过单纯校准消除；精确推断必须显式处理辅助结构，尤其在高精度测量（如中微子振荡、暗物质直接探测）中需避免基于数据依赖协方差的高斯重构。

Abstract: A covariance matrix is often used as a compact surrogate for the statistical model of a measurement. We show that this replacement can change not only a fitted value or its uncertainty, but also the likelihood-ratio ordering of the experiment itself. In a tractable correlated-measurement model, the data separate into an informative component and an ancillary residual disagreement. Exact likelihood inference therefore does not use that disagreement, whereas data-dependent covariance matrices and Gaussian reconstructions after nonlinear transformations can reintroduce it into parameter inference.
  We quantify the resulting change by the probability mass of the symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions - the fraction of repeated experiments for which the confidence decision about a tested parameter value changes. In the small-uncertainty regime the ordering discrepancy is generically first order in the total relative uncertainty, whereas conventional and ancillary-conditioned coverage defects begin at second order. At 'coverage-blind' points the leading coverage difference vanishes while the ordering discrepancy remains nonzero. In a representative blind case with 10% total relative uncertainty, about 7.6% of experiments change their confidence decision despite exact calibration of both procedures.
  The same mechanism connects Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle to the classical literature on ancillarity and relevant subsets: a covariance approximation can manufacture inferential relevance for a goodness-of-fit statistic that is ancillary in the true model. We show directly how this changes the confidence interval reported for the same informative content, and extend the analysis beyond equal statistical uncertainties and beyond the two-measurement case.

</details>


### [6] [A heterogeneous and vectorized sequence for the HL-LHC full tracking reconstruction of the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20135)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了CMS实验Phase-2升级中用于在线事件重建和主离线跟踪的新基准跟踪策略，融合GPU优化（Patatrack、LST）与CPU向量化（mkFit）算法，并引入位移顶点跟踪和机器学习以应对HL-LHC高事例重叠带来的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）下每束团交叉产生大量同时碰撞（pile-up）所导致的前所未有的计算挑战，需提升跟踪效率、精度与物理覆盖能力。

Method: 构建混合跟踪序列：集成GPU并行算法（Patatrack、LST）与CPU向量化算法（mkFit），并嵌入 displaced tracking 和机器学习增强模块，统一应用于在线重建与主离线迭代跟踪。

Result: 显著降低计算资源需求，同时提升物理性能——特别是对长寿命粒子、弱衰变顶点等位移轨迹的重建能力，并为ML驱动的模式识别与参数估计提供可扩展框架。

Conclusion: 该混合异构跟踪架构确立了CMS Phase-2跟踪的基准方案，兼顾实时性、精度与可扩展性，是支撑HL-LHC时代前沿物理分析的关键基础设施。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new baseline strategy for the Phase-2 tracking of the CMS experiment for online event reconstruction, and for the main iteration of offline tracking. This tracking sequence takes advantage of the combination of cutting-edge tracking algorithms that are either optimized for parallel execution on GPUs (Patatrack and LST), or vectorized for efficient CPU performance (mkFit). Such a combined approach offers an effective solution to deal with the unprecedented computational challenges caused by the large number of simultaneous collisions per bunch crossing expected at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). The proposed combination not only reduces the computational resource requirements but also enhances the physics reach by incorporating displaced tracking and increasingly leveraging machine learning techniques.

</details>

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII在√s=3.08 GeV处观测到显著的双s̄s产生，测得σ(e⁺e⁻→φ s̄s + X)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ + X) = (40.4±1.7±1.5)%，通过识别φ与含s̄s的末态（如η, η′, KK, KK*, K⁰K̄⁰等）；该结果与双c̄c产率相当，挑战现有QCD强子化理论预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QCD强子化机制中奇异夸克对产生的动力学，特别是与粲夸克对类比的双重味产生截面，以约束非微扰QCD模型和碎裂函数。

Method: 基于BESIII在√s=3.08 GeV采集的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，通过重建φ介子（→K⁺K⁻）并联合识别含s̄s的伴随强子系统X(s̄s)，包括η/η′及多种奇异介子对（K⁺K⁻、K⁺K*⁻、K⁻K*⁺、K⁰K̄⁰、K⁰K̄*⁰+c.c.），采用包含效率修正与背景减除的截面比测量方法。

Result: 测得双s̄s关联产生截面比为(40.4±1.7_stat±1.5_syst)%，显著高于多数理论预言，且与Belle/BaBar测得的双c̄c产率量级一致。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示奇异夸克对在e⁺e⁻→hadrons过程中存在强关联产生机制，表明当前QCD强子化模型（如Lund碎裂、string fragmentation）对s̄s对的多重产生压制可能被高估，需引入夸克-胶子集体效应或色相干修饰。

Abstract: We report the observation of significant double-$s\bar{s}$ production in the $e^+e^-$ continuum, based on the measurement of prompt $φ$ mesons produced in association with hadrons containing an $s$ quark or an $s\bar{s}$ pair. In an analysis of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII experiment at $\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}$, the ratio $σ(e^+e^- \to φ s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / σ(e^+e^-\rightarrowφ+\textrm{anything})$ is determined to be $(40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%$ by detecting and measuring $e^+e^-\toφ+ X(s\bar{s})$, where $X(s\bar{s})$ denotes an $η$ meson, an $η^{\prime}$ meson, or one of the strange-meson pairs $K^+K^-$, $K^+K^{*-}$, $K^-K^{*+}$, $K^0\bar{K}^{0}$, and $K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}$. The level of double-$s\bar{s}$ production is in line with the double-$c\bar{c}$ production reported by the Belle and \babar\ collaborations, for which theoretical calculations predict lower rates. The experimental measurement of double $s\bar{s}$ production at BESIII can shed light on the understanding of quark hadronization and QCD.

</details>

### [8] [Topological entanglement entropy in 3D gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19247)
*Vijay Balasubramanian,Charlie Cummings*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了三维引力中满足微分同胚不变性约束及含物质效应的张量网络态的熵公式，并论证其在小但有限牛顿常数下等价于量子极值面公式，且不依赖于时间对称性；推导基于正则量子化而非路径积分，并讨论了推广至紧致体区的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立符合广义相对论基本对称性（如微分同胚不变性）且能统一描述引力与物质的量子态模型，并给出其纠缠熵的普适表达式。

Method: 采用正则量子化方法构造满足约束方程的3D引力张量网络态，结合时空度规可逆性条件，推导其冯·诺依曼熵；未使用欧几里得路径积分。

Result: 得到一个适用于一般（非时间对称）时空的熵公式，该公式在小但有限 $G_N$ 下与量子极值面公式一致，并可延拓至紧致体区域。

Conclusion: 该张量网络模型成功实现了对量子引力中几何-纠缠对应关系的构造性实现，为AdS/CFT中的RT/HRT公式提供了非路径积分框架下的微观基础。

Abstract: We derive an entropy formula for a recently proposed tensor network model of states in 3D gravity which satisfy the constraint equations of diffeomorphism invariance, and including the effects of matter. After accounting for the invertibility of the spacetime metric, we argue that this formula matches the quantum extremal surface formula (for small but finite $G_N$), and is not restricted to time symmetric spacetimes. We derive the result via canonical quantization without relying on path integrals, and discuss an extension to compact bulk subregions.

</details>


### [9] [Cosmological Correlators from Scattering Amplitudes: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19252)
*Soner Albayrak,Savan Kharel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了如何将现代散射振幅方法（如自旋螺旋度、壳上递推、双拷贝、正几何、广义幺正性）推广至宇宙学背景，特别是通过单次解析延拓连接到欧氏反德西特（AdS）空间，并在动量空间中构建晚期边界上的原初统计描述；核心是总能量奇异点对应平直空间散射振幅，工具涵盖宇宙学多面体、壳上递推与弯曲空间双拷贝，并延伸至圈图、软极限、跃迁振幅及(A)dS切割规则，最终关联到暴胀观测量与宇宙学光学定理、宇宙学Bootstrap、宇宙学对撞机等前沿纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统S矩阵工具不适用于宇宙学相关函数（因无渐近态），但CMB与大尺度结构的原初统计信息编码于晚期宇宙波函数系数构成的相关函数中，亟需发展类S矩阵的计算框架。

Method: 基于晚期边界动量空间框架，以总能量（外腿动量模之和）为关键控制参数；从宇宙波方程出发，结合解析延拓至Euclidean AdS，运用宇宙学多面体、壳上递推、弯曲空间双拷贝、正几何与广义幺正性等现代振幅技术。

Result: 系统构建了适用于标量、规范场与引力子的树级宇宙学关联函数计算方法；揭示了不同自旋与维度间的普适关系；实现了绕过体积分的高效计算；拓展至圈图、软极限、跃迁振幅与(A)dS切割规则；建立了与暴胀观测量及宇宙学光学定理、Bootstrap、宇宙学对撞机的深刻联系。

Conclusion: 现代散射振幅方法已成功移植并深化为宇宙学原初非高斯性的统一计算范式，其核心是动量空间总能量结构与解析延拓桥梁；该框架不仅提供高效计算工具，更揭示了dS/AdS/CFT、有效场论与观测宇宙学之间的深层统一性，标志着宇宙学进入‘振幅时代’。

Abstract: The primordial statistics underlying the CMB and large-scale structure are encoded in late-time cosmological correlation functions (constructed from the coefficients of the wavefunction of the universe). They are the closest analogue cosmology has to an $S$-matrix, yet the standard $S$-matrix toolkit does not directly apply. This review explains how the modern scattering-amplitudes program (spinor helicity, on-shell recursion, the double copy, positive geometry, and generalized unitarity) has nevertheless been extended to cosmology and, through a single analytic continuation, to Euclidean anti-de Sitter space, where the same objects appear as holographic boundary correlators and are often cleanest to compute. The organizing framework is momentum space on the late-time boundary, where the primordial statistics are simplest and where the total energy of the external legs, the sum of their momentum magnitudes, controls the analytic structure. At the total-energy singularity, the flat-space scattering amplitude emerges. We develop this toolkit starting from the wave equation and use it to compute tree-level scalar, gauge-field, and graviton correlators, exposing relations across spins and dimensions. We then show how bulk integrals can be bypassed using cosmological polytopes, on-shell recursion, and curved-space versions of the double copy, before extending the discussion to loops, soft limits, transition amplitudes, and (A)dS cutting rules. Finally, we connect these structures to inflationary observables and to three allied programs: the cosmological optical theorem, the cosmological bootstrap, and the cosmological collider. We conclude with a map of the current frontier and a set of open and interesting problems.

</details>


### [10] [Deterministic Hypergraph SYK: Melonic Dominance and Maximal Chaos Without Disorder Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19255)
*Shiroman Prakash*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无序平均的稀疏确定性超图SYK模型（hSYK），在满足几何与谱展开条件时，其两点函数、四点函数及OTOC行为均复现了SYK模型的关键特征：melonic可解性、最大混沌、Schwarzian低能有效作用量，且具备量子模拟友好性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统SYK模型依赖无序平均的局限，构造一个确定性、稀疏、可量子模拟的模型，同时保留melonic结构与最大混沌等核心物理性质。

Method: 构建q-一致、d-正则超图上的Majorana费米子模型（hSYK），耦合为均匀正值；利用超图谱理论分析两点/四点函数；推导bilocal有效作用量与OTOC的line-graph随机游走表示；结合精确对角化验证标度律、能级统计与空间衰减。

Result: hSYK在大d极限下复现SYK的melonic Schwinger-Dyson方程（O(d⁻¹)修正）、相同主导bilocal有效作用量；四点函数分解为时间SYK ladder核与line-graph随机游走；有限line-graph谱隙控制OTOC中最大混沌模的可见性，并排除除Schwarzian外的额外轻模；数值验证melonic两点标度、单实例Wigner-Dyson统计及图调控的OTOC空间衰减。

Conclusion: hSYK是首个无需无序平均、稀疏且确定性的模型，完整实现SYK的核心melonic可解性与最大混沌特性，为量子模拟提供了理想候选平台。

Abstract: We show that SYK-like melonic solvability and maximal chaos survive in a sparse deterministic model, without disorder averaging. Our deterministic hypergraph SYK model (hSYK) consists of $N$ Majorana fermions on a $q$-uniform, $d$-regular interaction hypergraph with only $O(dN)$ interaction terms and uniform positive couplings. Under explicit geometric and spectral expansion conditions, its two-point function satisfies the standard melonic Schwinger-Dyson equation up to $O(d^{-1})$ corrections, while the leading bilocal effective action coincides with that of ordinary SYK at the dominant large-$d$ saddle. Its connected four-point function factorizes into a temporal SYK ladder kernel and a line-graph random walk. A finite line-graph spectral gap controls the visibility of the maximally chaotic uniform mode in generic localized OTOCs and, by gapping non-uniform spatial modes in the bilocal spectrum, excludes additional light fluctuation modes beyond the Schwarzian. Exact diagonalization further shows melonic two-point scaling, single-instance Wigner-Dyson statistics, and the predicted graph-controlled spatial decay of OTOCs. The model therefore offers a disorder-free realization of the essential melonic and chaotic features of SYK, in a form more amenable to quantum simulation.

</details>


### [11] [Schwarzschild Black Hole in Uniform Magnetic Field in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19261)
*Liang Ma,Peng-Yu Wu,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过标准解生成技术，在Kaluza-Klein理论中构造了外加均匀磁场中的Kerr黑洞，并推广至一般弦论启发的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton（EMD）理论，得到浸没于外磁场中的Schwarzschild黑洞精确静态解；进一步构建含电、磁双场的EMD电真空解，推广至多Maxwell场与多标量场情形，并揭示其运动方程可约化为Toda型方程，从而获得对应所有秩-2李群的解析电真空解及其浸没Schwarzschild黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson（KBR）解嵌入更一般的弦论启发EMD理论框架，并系统构造含外场的黑洞精确解，以探索高维/标量耦合引力中电磁结构与黑洞物理的普适性。

Method: 采用标准解生成技术（如Weyl变换、U-duality类映射或σ模型方法）从KK理论出发构造背景解；利用EMD理论的缩放对称性与靶空间对称性，将Kerr或Schwarzschild解嵌入外场；通过多标量-多矢量场系统的约化，导出Toda型运动方程并求解。

Result: 1) KK理论中带外磁场的Kerr解；2) 一般EMD理论中Schwarzschild黑洞浸没于均匀磁场的静态精确解（含KBR极限）；3) 含电、磁双场的EMD电真空解；4) 多Maxwell场与多稀子场推广下的Toda型约化；5) 对应A2、B2、G2等秩-2李群的解析电真空及其浸没Schwarzschild黑洞解。

Conclusion: EMD理论中存在丰富且可解析处理的电真空结构，其几何与规范场构型由李代数Toda系统完全刻画；Schwarzschild黑洞可稳定浸没于这些高对称电真空背景中，为研究强场量子引力效应与AdS/CFT对应中的边界场构型提供精确模型。

Abstract: We obtain the Kerr black hole in an external uniform magnetic field in the Kaluza-Klein (KK) theory via a standard solution-generating technique. Using this as our starting point, we construct exact static solutions describing a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in the external uniform magnetic field in general string-inspired Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theories, thereby embedding the static Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (KBR) solution into the general framework of EMD theories. We also construct electrovacuum solutions involving both electric and magnetic fields in EMD theories and generalize the system to include mutltiple Maxwell fields and dilatons. These equations of motion can be reduced to Toda-like equations, allowing us to obtain analytic electrovacua associated with Toda equations of all rank-2 Lie groups. We further construct exact solutions of a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in these electrovacua.

</details>


### [12] [Tameness and Complexity in Quantum Field Theory and Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19262)
*Mick van Vliet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了‘驯顺性’（tameness）与‘有限性’（finiteness）在物理数学结构中的基础作用，以o-极小理论（o-minimality）为框架，运用sharp o-极小性量化物理对象的复杂度，覆盖量子力学波函数、经典场构型、零维QFT非微扰观测量、宇宙学关联函数及有效场论拉格朗日量，并指出其与量子引力中有限性的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理定律背后的数学结构常表现出某种‘有限性’或‘可控复杂性’，而传统分析难以严格刻画该性质；o-极小理论提供了一种模型论意义上‘驯顺几何’的精确语言，可用于形式化这一物理直觉。

Method: 采用sharp o-极小性（sharp o-minimality）作为复杂度测度工具，在o-极小结构下系统分析多类物理对象的驯顺性，包括解析延拓性、可定义性、参数依赖阶数及几何奇点结构。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $248$ keV, motivating interpretations in terms of endothermic dark matter (DM). We investigate this signal in Higgs-coupled minimal dark matter (HC-MDM), where the same electroweak interaction responsible for the terrestrial recoil also induces DM capture in the Sun and a potentially observable high-energy neutrino signal. We independently determine the mass splittings required to reproduce the LZ event and perform an improved calculation of Solar capture and post-capture evolution. Our treatment includes natural isotope mixtures, exact inelastic kinematics, nuclear form factors, finite-temperature nuclear motion near kinematic closure, orbital cooling through loop-induced elastic scattering, and an explicit capture--annihilation equilibrium test. The resulting Solar predictions are numerically stable and robust against the astrophysical and nuclear variations considered. At early times, longitudinal expansion suppresses this coupling, and different spherical harmonic sectors follow their homogeneous attractors. We find that, at later times and sufficiently small $k$, this gradient coupling drives the system toward a global attractor manifold spanned by hydrodynamic sound and shear modes, on a timescale $τ_D$ that depends on both the azimuthal harmonic $m$ and $kτ_R$. Abstract: Discrete energy-level structures in Coulomb-like localized bound states are conventionally obtained by direct diagonalization of the Schrödinger equation or by semiclassical approximations that fail at low energies. Using Kustaanheimo--Stiefel regularization, we map negative-energy Kepler orbits to four-dimensional isotropic harmonic oscillators. The group composition law of the symplectic flow determines an integral kernel built from the classical two-point action and Van Vleck amplitude, whose infinitesimal generator satisfies an exact linear evolution equation---a property we term \textit{quadratic closure}. Imposing three geometric constraints---decay at infinity, regularity at the origin, and fiber invariance under the Hopf fibration---we recover the exact $E_n/E_1=1/n^2$ scaling and $g_n=n^2$ degeneracy. The phase scale $α$ sets the action unit; all spectral structural features are independent of its value. This framework offers a symmetry-transparent route to spectral data: for quadratic Hamiltonians, the classical kernel incurs zero semiclassical error even at ground-state energies, and the geometric constraints directly identify symmetry-protected degeneracies without diagonalization. We apply the method to shallow impurity states, moiré superlattices, and semiclassical transport, demonstrating how geometric screening rules simplify spectral analysis in condensed-matter environments.

Abstract: This work analyzes the spectral zeta function associated with regular and singular Sturm--Liouville operators endowed with $N$ generalized point potentials. The cumulative character of the framework concerns the response of the combined state to variation of externally prescribed conditions. For the special case of an initially perfect body, loss of stability may produce successive branches with defect and then source participation. Such a sequence is a bifurcation property of the selected initial state and constitutive energy, rather than a defining hierarchy of the framework. For the stable quartic energy considered, the gradient terms regularize nonuniform perturbations but do not by themselves generate a finite-wavelength localization instability on an already stable three-component branch. About a mass $M$, this carries an encoding area $A_E = λ\varphi \ell_p^2 = λR_g r \le A$ in a UV-IR consistent coupling $\ell_p^2= G\hbar/c^3$ to its Compton phase $\varphi = (mc/\hbar)r \propto 1/\hbar$. Here, $λ= 8π$ is fixed by the Newtonian limit at large distances and the saturation limit $A_E=A$ at horizon about $M$ based on the non-local extension of the equivalence principle in Rindler spacetime, without using the Einstein equations. In spherical symmetry, this recovers the Schwarzschild metric from a geometric embedding of $p=A_E/A$, which is directly observable in gravitational lensing. The result shows a non-thermal origin of the scaling dimension of two of gravitational phase space, previously derived based on the second law of thermodynamics by Bekenstein (1973), with possible implications for cosmological spacetime.

Conclusion: Kerr-Vaidya背景下Penrose过程的能量提取效率对质量流失率高度敏感；有效自旋漂移是主导动力学效应；α_crit(a)单调递减表明高自旋黑洞更易因过快质量损失而丧失能量提取能力；单粒子近似在多数区域仍具合理性，但须谨慎处理视界邻域与面积约束。

Abstract: We present a corrected numerical investigation of the Penrose process in the Kerr-Vaidya metric for a rotating black hole losing mass at constant rate. We fix two errors from an earlier version: an incorrect metric component g_r phi (verified by transformation from Boyer-Lindquist coordinates) and the omission of the Wald/Christodoulou area theorem in the split optimization. Using the standard single-particle treatment (energy and angular momentum conserved at the split, but not the escaping fragment's radial momentum), we compute optimal trajectories and the bounded energy gain Delta E = E3 - E1 versus spin a/M and mass-loss rate alpha. For static Kerr holes, Delta E rises with spin from about 0.106 M at a/M = 0.8 to 0.245 M at a/M = 0.99. In the dynamic case, holding a fixed while m(v) decreases causes the effective spin a/m(v) to drift strongly toward extremality (from 0.81 to 0.9999), a geometric effect that makes static-dynamic comparisons at equal nominal a/M misleading. We map the critical mass-loss rate alpha_crit(a) above which no viable extraction is found (using m >= 0), obtaining alpha_crit(0.90) ~ 0.036, alpha_crit(0.95) ~ 0.019 and alpha_crit(0.99) ~ 0.0038, decreasing with spin. A check with full four-momentum conservation recovers the Bardeen-Press-Teukolsky bound near the horizon, but escaping solutions are rare away from it; thus our main results refer to the relaxed single-particle formulation common in the literature.

Abstract: We construct bosonic stars in the Einstein-Weinberg-Salam theory, namely the bosonic sector of the electroweak Standard Model minimally coupled to Einstein's theory of gravity. Abstract: We study machine-learning detection of controlled beyond-General-Relativity (beyond-GR) deviations in gravitational-wave signals, using both synthetic aLIGO-PSD noise and real LIGO H1 detector strain. The central result is a quantitative detectability curve as a function of $β$. The threshold value is specific to the quadratic-in-time modulation form adopted here and should not be interpreted as a generic constraint on beyond-GR parameters. Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) memory is a permanent change in spacetime geometry induced by a burst of gravitational waves. It is theoretically interesting for its connection to asymptotic symmetries, but has not yet been observed. The upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is predicted to detect memory directly in loud events from supermassive black hole (SMBH) mergers. We assess the prospects for the cumulative effect of memory contributions as a stochastic GW memory background (SGWMB). Using two SMBH population models, we recover previously obtained single-event SNR forecasts and compute the power spectrum of the unresolved background, using numerical-relativity waveforms for modeling the memory signal during each event. We find the predicted spectrum is significantly smaller than ones found by modeling the memory waveform with a Heaviside function, with expected SNRs of order $O(0.1-10)$. We further find that our models favor a highly non-Gaussian ("popcorn") spectrum, demonstrating that the background may not appear as the union of continuous, overlapping signals, but rather as a collection of intermittent bursts. In both measures, we demonstrate that any current prediction of the spectrum is heavily dependent on the choice of population model. We interpret these findings in the light of the future LISA mission and emphasize their importance for properly handling memory within the LISA global fit.

Abstract: In single-field inflation, linear perturbations originate as quantum fluctuations and undergo a quantum-to-classical transition on super-horizon scales as the decaying mode of the comoving curvature perturbation $\mathcal{R}$ is suppressed. This transition provides a condition for the validity of a classical stochastic description of $\mathcal{R}$. We characterize classicality through the suppression of the quantum commutator relative to phase-space variances and the evolution of the squeezing parameters. In the Starobinsky model, we show that this transition becomes mode-dependent at linear order due to the transient ultra-slow-roll (USR) phase. This is because the decaying mode transiently grows during the USR phase modifying the squeezing dynamics, and shifting the classicalization time depending on when modes exit the horizon relative to the USR phase. We then study the implications of this mode-dependent transition for the stochastic description of perturbations. Using the Green's function method, we show that the stochastic spectrum reproduces the power spectrum of the quantum state initialized in the Bunch-Davies vacuum, independently of the mode-dependent transition when the gradient term is retained in the Langevin equation, for both white and colored noise coarse-graining. In contrast, ignoring the gradient term in the Langevin equation introduces an explicit dependence on the coarse-graining parameter $σ$. Homogeneous matching procedure can be used to set mode-dependent $σ(k)$ that reproduces the power spectrum of the quantum state at the end of inflation. Crucially, however, we show that the matching times precede the classicalization times for modes near the USR transition. Therefore, the transient USR phase in this model leads to a mode-dependent condition for when linear curvature perturbations can be described as classical stochastic noise.

We discuss prospects for extending this approach to other compact objects.

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations of binary neutron star mergers probe the neutron star equation of state through finite-size effects on the waveform. These include the tidal deformation of the stars due to their respective companion, but for spinning neutron stars also include the spin-induced quadrupole moment of the stars. Typically, to avoid introducing additional equation of state dependent parameters, waveform models relate this quadrupole moment to the tidal deformability through so-called quasi-universal relations. These are derived under the slow-rotation approximation, which at larger spins, i.e., for millisecond pulsars, becomes unreliable. We attempt to extend the existing quasi-universal relations by explicitly accounting for the spin, calibrating it against numerically computed quadrupole moments across a broad set of equations of state and spins $0.15 \leq |χ| \leq 0.6$ with the $χ\rightarrow 0$ limit enforced by construction. The resulting spin-extended relation reduces the median absolute percentage error relative to the slow-rotation relation by 66\% across this range. Parameter estimation using the spin-extended relation reduces biases in recovered intrinsic parameters in some cases, though biases from mass-spin degeneracies dominate largely when a population is considered. These results provide a more accurate description of the spin-induced quadrupole moment for spinning neutron star binaries, relevant for waveform modelling with third-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

Abstract: We study spontaneous scalarization near and at extremality in Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory. As Reissner-Nordström (RN) extremality is approached from below, the nonextremal scalarization existence curves collapse toward a common critical threshold $α_c=1/4$ given by the Breitenlohner-Freedman bound for the near-horizon throat (AdS$_2\times S^2$) of its extremal black hole. For a polynomial coupling, we construct extremal black holes with nonconstant scalar hair, satisfying $M^2+Q_s^2=Q^2$, which places them in the overcharged region $Q/M>1$ beyond the RN bound. A common near-region phase mechanism predicts accumulation toward the RN extremality from both sides, yielding Miransky-type scaling. In the extremal sector, the logarithmic phase interval doubles, halving the exponent which denotes the geometric spacing of successive node scales.

Abstract: We investigate traversable wormholes supported by Casimir vacuum energy with second-order generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) corrections. For two representative GUP models, we derive higher-order corrections to the Casimir energy and construct exact wormhole solutions in general relativity. The resulting geometries satisfy the throat, flare-out, and asymptotic-flatness conditions. Higher-order corrections modify the wormhole mass and reduce the exotic matter required, although the null and weak energy conditions remain violated near the throat. We further analyze weak gravitational lensing and gravitational-wave echoes, finding model-dependent signatures that may distinguish different GUP realizations. By relating the dimensionless parameter used in the solutions to the conventional phenomenological GUP parameter, we show that its physical interpretation depends strongly on the throat radius. Most current experimental bounds favor near-Planckian throats for appreciable GUP corrections, while macroscopic throats generally require much weaker corrections. These results provide a framework for confronting GUP-corrected Casimir wormholes with laboratory constraints and astrophysical observations.

Motivation: 澄清框架不变标量-张量引力热力学是否仅限于爱因斯坦帧类完美流体描述，拓展其热力学结构的几何与物理自由度。

Result: 建立了Jordan-frame-like不变热力学表示，其中引力作用具Brans-Dicke-Bergmann-Wagoner形式且所有场与函数均为运动学不变量；有效应力张量在一般观测者系下含非零热流与各向异性应力；在Ψ-共动系下恢复不变形式的第一定律关系K_J T_J = −\dotΨ/(κ²Ψ)与剪切粘滞系数η_J = \dotΨ/(2κ²Ψ)，并在均匀各向同性情形中获得体粘滞通道。

Conclusion: 框架不变标量-张量热力学具有双重不变组织结构：不仅支持Einstein-frame-like零温完美流体描述，也容许Jordan-frame-like非完美流体解释，表明其热力学本性更丰富，不依赖于特定共形帧的选择。

Abstract: It has been shown that the thermodynamics of frame-invariant scalar-tensor gravity admits an Einstein-frame-like invariant representation, in which the scalar sector is minimally coupled and behaves as a perfect fluid with vanishing temperature. We show that this is not the only invariant thermodynamic organization available. Using instead the invariant matter metric, we construct a Jordan-frame-like invariant representation in which matter is minimally coupled and the gravitational action takes the Brans-Dicke-Bergmann-Wagoner form with invariant fields $Ψ=\mathcal I_1^{-1}$, $\mathcal U(Ψ)$, and $ω_{\mathrm{inv}}(Ψ)$. In this representation the metric field equations retain the Hessian sector characteristic of Jordan-frame scalar-tensor gravity. A $1+3$ decomposition of the corresponding effective stress tensor yields nonvanishing heat flux and anisotropic stress in a generic congruence. In the $Ψ$-comoving frame, the usual first-order thermodynamic identifications are recovered in invariant form, including $K_{\mathrm J}T_{\mathrm J}=-\dotΨ/(κ^2Ψ)$ and $η_{\mathrm J}=\dotΨ/(2κ^2Ψ)$, with the bulk channel obtained in homogeneous and isotropic sectors. Thus, frame-invariant scalar-tensor thermodynamics is not intrinsically restricted to an Einstein-frame-like perfect-fluid description: it also admits a Jordan-frame-like invariant formulation in which the effective imperfect-fluid interpretation is manifest.

</details>


### [100] [Entropy production during reheating after inflation and General Relativistic Entropic Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20242)
*Juan Garcia-Bellido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过经典统计格点模拟（CosmoLattice）计算预热过程中场涨落的粗粒化相空间熵，量化其不可逆性；引入广义相对论熵致加速（GREA）框架，将熵产耦合回宇宙膨胀动力学，发现熵致加速度与能量耗散（而非熵本身）直接相关，并在不同预热机制（自旋odal、湍流、共振）中表现出显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预热是暴胀后由相干低熵标量场凝聚态向非平衡粒子浴转化的关键不可逆过程，但其熵产动力学及对背景宇宙演化的影响尚未被定量刻画。

Method: 采用CosmoLattice进行经典统计格点模拟，在自洽FLRW背景下重构各模态占据数n_k，计算相空间熵S=∑_k[(1+n_k)ln(1+n_k)−n_k ln n_k]；结合GREA框架，将熵产反馈至弗里德曼方程，修正尺度因子演化。

Result: 相空间熵S可清晰区分预热三阶段（共振主导、湍流主导、自旋odal），且ΔS为物理上稳健的可观测量；熵致加速度实质追踪热量转移，在tachyonic与quartic-turbulent模型中显著（O(1)能量碎片化），但在m²φ²共振模型中可忽略，尽管其熵产巨大；后续微扰重热主导宇宙总熵（S₀∼10⁸⁸），但不引发可观测加速度。

Conclusion: 预热的不可逆性可通过相空间熵严格量化；熵产本身不直接驱动宇宙加速，而能量向辐射的快速耗散（即热化速率）才是GREA效应的关键；该结果揭示了非平衡场论、引力动力学与热力学第二定律在早期宇宙中的深层耦合。

Abstract: Preheating transforms the coherent, low-entropy energy of the post-inflationary inflaton condensate into a far-from-equilibrium bath of field quanta, and is therefore an intrinsically irreversible process. We quantify that irreversibility directly, by computing the coarse-grained phase-space entropy of the field fluctuations from classical-statistical lattice simulations performed with CosmoLattice. For each field we reconstruct the mode occupation numbers $n_k$ and evaluate $S=\sum_k[(1+n_k)\ln(1+n_k)-n_k\ln n_k]$, following its growth through the different stages of reheating in a self-consistently expanding Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker background. We study three canonical models coupled to a daughter scalar $χ$ through $g^2φ^2χ^2$. We discuss the ultraviolet vacuum floor intrinsic to the lattice initial conditions and argue that the produced entropy $ΔS$ is the physically robust diagnostic. The phase-space entropy thus provides a compact, dynamical measure that cleanly separates the resonance-dominated, turbulence-dominated, and spinodal regimes of preheating. We couple this entropy production back into the expansion through the general relativistic entropic acceleration (GREA) framework, modifying the self-consistent scale-factor evolution of CosmoLattice to include the entropic force. The resulting acceleration tracks the heat (the energy transferred to particles) rather than the entropy itself: it is substantial in the tachyonic and quartic-turbulent cases, where an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ fraction of the coherent energy fragments into radiation, but negligible in the resonant $m^2φ^2$ model despite its copious entropy production. The slow perturbative reheating that follows generates the bulk of the cosmic entropy ($S_0 \sim10^{88}$) yet drives no appreciable acceleration and shuts off entirely at thermalization.

</details>


### [101] [Quasinormal modes of Schwarzschild--AdS black holes with a near-horizon reflective surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20382)
*Libo Xie,Liang-Bi Wu,Yu-Sen Zhou,Xi-Ning Dong,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有近事件视界部分反射面的Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的标量准正规模（QNM）谱，发现其在大实部区域呈现由腔长和表面反射率主导的等间距‘腔模’结构；大AdS半径下还出现受离心势垒与AdS势壁束缚的弱阻尼准束缚态分支；通过有限径向腔图像和WKB渐近分析给出物理解释，并揭示QNM谱对近视界边界条件（即视界修正效应）具有强敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS黑洞QNM谱对近视界物理修正（如量子引力效应或有效边界条件）的敏感性，探索类腔结构对模式谱的影响机制。

Method: 数值求解基于Chebyshev谱配置法的标量场波动方程；辅以WKB渐近分析与有限径向腔物理图像建模。

Result: 1) QNM谱在大实部区域呈现等间距腔模结构，间距与阻尼由腔长及反射率决定；2) 大AdS半径下出现弱阻尼准束缚态分支；3) WKB给出高实部模式的定量渐近描述；4) 近视界边界条件微小改变导致QNM谱显著变化。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的标量QNM谱强烈依赖于近视界边界条件，其谱结构可由有限径向腔模型有效刻画，表明QNM是探测视界附近物理修正的灵敏探针。

Abstract: We investigate the scalar quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum of Schwarzschild-AdS black holes with a partially reflective surface placed near the event horizon. The corresponding QNM problem is solved numerically using Chebyshev spectral collocation. The resulting spectra exhibit a characteristic cavity structure at sufficiently large real part, where the modes form approximately equally spaced sequences whose spacing and damping are controlled mainly by the cavity length and the surface reflectivity. For large AdS radius, an additional weakly damped quasibound-state branch appears due to trapping between the centrifugal barrier and the AdS potential wall. We show that these spectral features can be understood within a finite radial cavity picture. A WKB analysis provides a quantitative asymptotic description of the spectra which have high real parts. We find that changes in the near-horizon boundary condition can lead to substantial changes in the QNM spectrum, indicating the strong sensitivity of Schwarzschild--AdS QNMs to the effect of horizon correction.

</details>


### [102] [Gravity from Invariant Weyl-Integrable space-time (IWIST)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20619)
*José Edgar Madriz Aguilar,A. Bernal,M. Montes,J. Zamarripa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Weyl-可积时空（IWIST）的几何引力理论，通过Palatini变分原理动态确定背景几何；构造了在微分同胚与Weyl变换下均不变的作用量，并引入Weyl协变变分法；导出度规、Weyl标量场与Weyl规范场的场方程；在Einstein–Riemann标架下将标量场诠释为几何起源的物理自由度；并通过Weyl规范场与标量流耦合显式破缺Weyl对称性，得到一个更一般的、保持微分同胚协变但不具局域Weyl不变性的引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准广义相对论中背景几何人为预设的问题，从几何第一性原理出发构建具有Weyl对称性的引力理论，并探索该对称性破缺的自然机制以连接可观测物理。

Method: 采用Palatini变分方法，在Weyl-可积流形上构造Weyl协变作用量；引入Weyl协变散度定理推广；进行Weyl协变变分得到独立场方程；执行共形变换至Einstein–Riemann标架；通过最小耦合机制引入Weyl规范场与标量流的相互作用以破缺Weyl对称性。

Result: 1) 得到IWIST中度规、Weyl标量场φ及Weyl规范场A_μ的完整Weyl协变场方程；2) 证明Einstein–Riemann标架下有效度规为Riemannian，且φ成为动力学标量场；3) 构造显式Weyl对称性破缺项，导出含额外一般协变相互作用项的引力理论；4) 原Weyl不变模型为其对称性未破缺极限。

Conclusion: Weyl-可积几何为引力提供了比Riemann几何更基本的几何框架；Weyl对称性可作为指导原理构造引力理论，其自发或显式破缺能自然引出物理标量场与新相互作用；该框架统一了几何结构动力学与标量场物理，是通向尺度不变量子引力的可行路径之一。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a geometrically motivated formulation of gravity in an invariant Weyl--Integrable space-time (IWIST), in which the background geometry is not imposed a priori but is dynamically determined through the Palatini variational principle. For a scalar field non-minimally coupled to gravity action, the resulting compatibility condition defines a Weyl--Integrable geometry which is preserved by a local Weyl transformation of the metric and the geo\-me\-tri\-cal scalar field. We construct an action invariant under both diffeomorphisms and Weyl transformations and introduce a Weyl-covariant variational procedure based on an invariant extension of the divergence theorem. The corresponding field equations are obtained for the metric, the Weyl scalar, and the Weyl gauge field. We further formulate the theory in the Einstein--Riemann frame, where the effective metric is Riemannian and the scalar field is re\-inter\-pre\-ted as a phy\-si\-cal degree of freedom of geometric origin. Finally, we propose a geometrical mechanism for the explicit breaking of Weyl symmetry by introducing a coupling between the Weyl gauge field and a current constructed from the scalar sector. The resulting theory remains diffeomorphism covariant while departing from local Weyl invariance. The Einstein--Riemann representation of the broken theory contains an additional generally covariant interaction, making the Weyl-invariant model a particular limit of a more general gravitational theory.

</details>


### [103] [The return of Palatini inflationary attractors: Universal mapping of observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20622)
*Christian Dioguardi,Francesco Gianesello,Antonio Racioppi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini引力框架下单场慢滚暴胀模型中的ξ-吸引子类，该类模型中非最小耦合项与暴胀势能均由同一函数f(φ)决定；发现e折数在领头阶下与非最小耦合参数ξ无关，从而建立了非最小耦合与最小耦合暴胀可观测量之间的直接映射；强耦合极限下，张量-标量比被压低，而标量谱指数向更大值偏移，其偏移量仅依赖于原势能V(φ)对应的r。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非最小耦合暴胀模型在Palatini引力下的可观测预言，并建立其与最小耦合情形的系统性对应关系，以检验不同引力理论框架下暴胀模型的普适性与区分能力。

Method: 基于Palatini形式的引力作用量，对单场慢滚暴胀进行共形变换分析，导出约化标量场空间中的有效势与动能项，计算慢滚参数ε、η及可观测量n_s、r，利用e折数N对ξ的弱依赖性构造映射关系。

Result: 证明在ξ-吸引子类中，e折数N在O(1/ξ)精度下与ξ无关；由此得出n_s与r在非最小耦合和最小耦合情形间存在确定性映射：Δn_s ≈ r/8，且r_eff ∝ 1/ξ²；该结果不依赖于f(φ)的具体形式，仅由原始势V(φ)的r决定。

Conclusion: Palatini ξ-吸引子暴胀具有强模型无关性：其CMB可观测量由原始势V(φ)决定，非最小耦合仅起重标度与微扰修正作用；这为利用Planck/BICEP等数据约束引力理论与暴胀模型联合参数空间提供了简洁解析工具。

Abstract: We study single-field slow-roll inflation in the context of Palatini gravity for the class of non-minimally coupled $ξ$-attractors, i.e.,\ models where the same function $f(φ)$ fixes both the non-minimal coupling, $1+ξf(φ)$, and the inflationary potential, $V(φ) = V_0 f(φ)^2$. As is well-known, for an arbitrary $f(φ)$, the number of $e$-folds is, at leading order, independent of $ξ$. Thanks to this, we provide an immediate mapping between the observables of the non-minimally coupled setup and those of the minimally coupled one. In the strong coupling limit, the tensor-to-scalar ratio is, as usual, suppressed, while the scalar spectral index is shifted towards larger values, with the magnitude of the shift depending only on the tensor-to-scalar ratio associated with the original potential $V(φ)$.

</details>


### [104] [On the Extended Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson Spacetime: Two Black Holes and a Naked Singularity in Bertotti-Robinson Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20626)
*Yu-Sen Zhou,Wen-Tao Fu,Li-Ming Cao,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文引入了新的坐标系，以一致地延拓Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson时空，并揭示其包含两个共享非平凡拓扑外区的黑洞及一个裸环奇点；在静态正则极限下，该坐标系全局等价于平衡Alekseev--García几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson类时空的全局结构与奇点解析性，特别是Ω=0处的退化性问题，需构造能解析分辨Bertotti--Robinson型零无穷远的坐标系。

Method: 基于先前的倒易延拓（reciprocal continuation）框架，构造新型坐标系，使Ω=0两点被展开为完整的Bertotti--Robinson型零无穷远，并分析延拓后时空的因果结构、拓扑与奇点性质。

Result: 新坐标下，原退化点Ω=0被解析为两个完整的Bertotti--Robinson零无穷远；延拓时空具有双黑洞结构、共享非平凡拓扑外区，并含一裸环奇点；静态极限下全局等价于平衡Alekseev--García解。

Conclusion: 该坐标系提供了Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson类时空更完备的全局描述，凸显其与可积引力解（如Alekseev--García）的深层联系，并为研究带电旋转黑洞的极端极限与共形边界结构提供新工具。

Abstract: We introduce new coordinates that extend the Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson spacetime consistently with the previous reciprocal continuation. In these coordinates, the two points at $Ω=0$ are resolved into complete Bertotti--Robinson null infinities. The extended spacetime describes two black holes sharing a common exterior of nontrivial topology, together with a naked ring singularity. In the regular static limit, the new coordinates globally identify the spacetime with the balanced Alekseev--García geometry.

</details>


### [105] [Entropy, area, and the choice of regulator during gravitational collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20663)
*Jana N. Guenther,Christian Hoelbling,Sophie Mutzel,Lukas Varnhorst*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时形式主义，用于数值模拟球对称情形下标量量子场的引力坍缩；采用专为抵消协变点分裂所识别的能量-动量张量紫外发散（含对数发散）而设计的Pauli-Villars调节器；发现平直时空球形区域的纠缠熵主导项（正比于表面积）消失；初步数值结果表明，无质量标量场坍缩壳的面积归一化熵集中在壳上，且在演化过程中近似保持常数最大值，该值在连续极限下有限，且对调节器质量仅弱依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决球对称量子引力坍缩中能量-动量张量紫外发散问题，并准确计算动态背景下的纠缠熵演化行为。

Method: 构建实时间形式主义框架，结合球对称约化与Pauli-Villars正则化（适配协变点分裂识别的紫外结构，含log发散抵消），对无质量标量场坍缩进行数值求解。

Result: 1) 平直时空中球形区域的面积律纠缠熵主项严格为零；2) 坍缩壳的面积归一化熵局域于壳上，演化中维持近似恒定的最大值；3) 该最大值在连续极限下收敛、有限，且对Pauli-Villars质量参数弱依赖。

Conclusion: 该正则化方案能一致处理弯曲时空中的量子涨落与引力反馈；面积归一化熵在动力学坍缩中呈现类热、局域化行为，支持黑洞熵的几何起源猜想，但其有限性与弱调节器依赖暗示可能需进一步考虑更普适的紫外完备理论。

Abstract: We present a real time formalism to numerically describe the gravitational collapse of a scalar quantum field in the spherically symmetric case. We employ a Pauli-Villars regulator that is specifically designed to cancel the ultraviolet divergences in the energy-momentum tensor identified by covariant point splitting, including the logarithmic ones. Using this regulator, we find that the leading term of the entanglement entropy, which is proportional to the surface area, vanishes for a spherical region of flat spacetime. First numerical results for the dynamical case indicate that for a collapsing shell of a massless scalar field, the area normalized entropy is concentrated on the shell and has an approximately constant maximum value during time evolution. This maximum value appears to be finite in the continuum limit and only mildly dependent on the regulator mass.

</details>


### [106] [Spin-network states for the Bianchi I and IX cosmological models from quantum constrained symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20703)
*Matteo Bruno,Giovanni Montani,Edoardo Maria Panno*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在量子层面实现了Ashtekar变量下齐次SU(2)规范理论的规范固定条件，将其与标准minisuperspace宇宙学模型相联系；通过构造满足散度约束与对角约束的量子算符，并利用全息与通量正则化，证明特定齐次自旋网络态（Bianchi I/IX）满足这些量子约束，从而在量子水平上建立了完整SU(2)规范对称宇宙学与圈量子宇宙学（LQC）之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Ashtekar变量下具有完整SU(2)规范对称性的齐次宇宙学模型与标准圈量子宇宙学（LQC）之间的严格量子对应关系，弥补经典约化与量子理论之间的鸿沟。

Method: 引入并量子化两类经典约束：散度约束（实现齐次规范固定）与对角约束（保证度规对角性）；采用圈量子引力框架下的holonomy-flux代数进行正则化；在Reduced Quantum Loop Gravity框架下检验特定齐次自旋网络态对约束的满足性。

Result: 构造出适用于一般宇宙模型的散度与对角约束的量子算符；证明Bianchi I和Bianchi IX对应的齐次自旋网络态精确满足这些量子约束；揭示所选态具有有效阿贝尔结构，从而实现SU(2)规范理论向LQC的量子约化。

Conclusion: 首次在量子层面确立了具有完整SU(2)规范对称性的齐次Ashtekar宇宙学与标准圈量子宇宙学之间的等价性，为LQC提供了更坚实的全理论基础，并支持其作为量子引力宇宙学子理论的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we implement at the quantum level the gauge-fixing conditions that relate the homogeneous SU(2) gauge theory of Ashtekar variables, describing the classical cosmological sector, to the usual minisuperspace formulation. We impose the so-called divergence constraint, which fixes the gauge to the homogeneous one and, at the classical level, recovers a finite-dimensional phase space, together with the diagonal constraint, which imposes the diagonality of the metric and has already been extensively studied in the literature. We construct the corresponding quantum operators for general cosmological models and provide a suitable regularization in terms of holonomies and fluxes. We then show that a special class of homogeneous spin-network states describing the Bianchi I and Bianchi IX models satisfies the quantum gauge-fixing constraints, using techniques developed in Reduced Quantum Loop Gravity. This provides a quantum-level link between the cosmological theory with full SU(2) gauge symmetry and standard Loop Quantum Cosmology, by reason of the effective Abelian structure of the selected states.

</details>

### [107] [Symplectic lattice gauge theories in the Grid framework: domain wall fermions and continuum extrapolations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19930)
*Ed Bennett,Peter A. Boyle,Luigi Del Debbio,Niccolò Forzano,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C. -J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Gianmarco Simonetti,Davide Vadacchino,Alexis Verney-Provatas*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次使用域壁费米子（MDWF）在Sp(4)规范理论中进行格点数值研究，该理论含两个味的狄拉克费米子；通过Grid实现MDWF，系统测试并优化算法参数，测量轻介子质量与衰变常数，并外推至连续极限；相比Wilson费米子结果，MDWF在物理相关参数区域展现出数量级量级的连续极限收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sp(4)双味规范理论是复合Higgs模型和强相互作用大质量粒子（SIMP）暗物质模型的重要短距完备化候选；需高精度格点模拟以可靠提取低能强耦合可观测量（如介子谱、衰变常数），而传统Wilson费米子存在较大离散化误差。

Method: 采用Mobius域壁费米子（MDWF）公式，在Grid软件框架下实现；系统开展算法实现验证与MDWF作用量中5维域壁高度、移位参数等关键算法参数的优化；在多个格点耦合强度及中等费米子质量的组态系综上，计算最轻味介子（如π、K类似态）的质量m_π和衰变常数f_π，并执行a→0连续极限外推。

Result: MDWF方案在相同计算资源下，相较Wilson费米子，使物理观测量（如m_π、f_π）向连续极限的收敛速度提升约一个数量级；成功获得外推后的连续极限值，并与已有Wilson费米子结果形成系统性对比。

Conclusion: MDWF是Sp(4)双味理论格点研究的高效工具，显著压制O(a)离散化误差，为未来高精度复合动力学唯象学预言（如Higgs势形貌、暗物质热历史）提供了可靠且可扩展的数值基础。

Abstract: We report the results of the first numerical lattice study using domain-wall fermions in the Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two flavours of (Dirac) fermions, transforming in the fundamental representation of the gauge group. This theory plays a prominent role in the literature on extensions of the Standard Model with composite dynamics. It provides a short-distance completion for a class of composite Higgs models, or, alternatively, of dark matter models based on the strongly interacting massive particle paradigm. We adopt the Möbius formulation of domain-wall fermions (MDWF), implemented within the Grid software environment. We report the results of extensive tests of the algorithm implementation, and of the optimisation of the choices of algorithmic parameters appearing in the MDWF action. We then measure masses and decay constants of the lightest flavoured mesons in ensembles with moderately large fermion masses and several choices of lattice coupling, and perform an extrapolation to the continuum. We compare our results for the physical observables to published measurements obtained in the same field theory, but derived on the lattice by employing Wilson fermions. We demonstrate that, with the deployment of moderate computational resources, the MDWF formulation can yield order-of-magnitude gains in the approach to the continuum limit, in regions of physical parameter space relevant to phenomenological applications of this theory.

</details>


### [108] [Numerical Investigations of Phase Transitions in Lattice Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20536)
*Vamika Longia*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过两种互补的数值方法研究格点系统的相变：一是使用GPU加速的高阶张量重正化群（HOTRG）研究二维广义XY模型的多相结构与相边界；二是构建并验证基于欧氏作用量梯度与Hessian的构型温度估计器，用于U(1)规范理论中热化采样的独立诊断与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值工具在解析细微临界现象方面仍缺乏高精度能力，亟需发展鲁棒、高效且可交叉验证的相变分析方法。

Method: 1) GPU加速的高阶张量重正化群（HOTRG）用于二维广义XY模型的热力学极限下相图刻画；2) 基于欧氏格点作用量一阶与二阶导数构造构型温度估计器，并在紧致U(1)格点规范理论中进行基准测试。

Result: HOTRG成功分辨出广义XY模型的铁磁、向列与顺磁三相及其精确相边界；构型温度估计器被证实为低开销、算法无关的热化诊断工具，可实时识别蒙特卡洛采样病态行为。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法与构型温度估计器构成互补框架：前者提供高精度相结构解析能力，后者提供普适性热化验证与运行时监控能力，二者协同增强格点场论中临界现象研究的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: The study of phase transitions plays an important role in understanding qualitative changes in the behaviour of physical systems at criticality. Despite decades of progress, there is still a strong demand for high-precision numerical tools capable of resolving subtle critical phenomena. Motivated by this need, in this thesis, we present two complementary numerical investigations of phase transitions in lattice systems. The first uses GPU-accelerated higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG) techniques to study the two-dimensional generalized XY model, characterizing its ferromagnetic, nematic, and paramagnetic phases and mapping their phase boundaries using thermodynamic observables in the thermodynamic limit. The second develops and benchmarks a configurational temperature estimator, constructed from gradients and Hessians of the Euclidean lattice action, in compact U(1) lattice gauge theories. On one hand, tensor network methods capture rich phase structures when truncation and finite-bond effects are adequately controlled. On the other hand, the configurational temperature estimator provides an independent, low-overhead means of validating thermal sampling across different algorithms and models, and can also be used as a runtime diagnostic to identify sampling pathologies before large-scale production runs.

</details>


### [109] [Computability of GPDs near $x=\pmξ$ in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20545)
*Yushan Su,Xiangdong Ji,Yizhuang Liu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文改进了格点QCD中广义部分子分布（GPDs）的大动量有效理论（LaMET）处理，放宽了对部分硬标度 $2|x\pm\xi|P^z$ 的要求，使 $x \sim \pm\xi$ 区域可计算；通过完整的一圈匹配重分析已有格点数据，首次在格点上观测到GPDs在 $x=\pm\xi$ 处的阈值行为（函数连续但导数不连续），并给出分布振幅类区域的重要预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LaMET框架要求所有硬标度（包括 $2|x\pm\xi|P^z$ 和 $2|1\pm x|P^z$）远大于 $\Lambda_{\rm QCD}$，导致关键的 $x\sim\pm\xi$ 区域（即partonic threshold region）不可达，限制了GPDs的完整重构。

Method: 采用大动量有效理论（LaMET）结合完整的一圈匹配计算，系统分析硬标度依赖性，识别并放松对 $2|x\pm\xi|P^z$ 的强约束条件；对已有格点QCD数据进行重新匹配与外推。

Result: 证明当 $\xi$ 较大时，$2|x\pm\xi|P^z \gg \Lambda_{\rm QCD}$ 的要求可显著弱化；在格点上首次明确观测到GPDs在 $x=\pm\xi$ 处的连续性与导数不连续性（即partonic threshold behavior）；获得分布振幅类区域（$|x|<|\xi|$）的可靠预言，实现夸克与反夸克PDF-like行为的平滑连接。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了LaMET在GPDs计算中的传统标度限制，拓展了格点可及的 $x$–$\xi$ 相空间范围，为从第一性原理提取GPDs、特别是其奇点结构和手征奇偶性提供关键理论支撑与数值验证。

Abstract: In lattice QCD computations of generalized parton distributions (GPDs), the large momentum expansion generally requires all hard scales, $2|x\pmξ|P^z$ and $2|1\pm x|P^z$, to be much larger than $Λ_{\rm QCD}$. We show that this condition can be relaxed for $2|x\pmξ|P^z$ at large $ξ$, making the important $x\sim\pmξ$ regions accessible to lattice calculations and considerably expanding the region of computability. Revisiting previous lattice results with complete one-loop matching, we obtain the expected partonic threshold behavior---GPDs continuous at $x=\pmξ$ but with discontinuous derivatives---which has not previously been observed on the lattice. We thus obtain, for the first time, important prediction for GPDs in the distribution-amplitude-like region, which smoothly connects the quark and antiquark PDF-like behaviors.

</details>
